<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（36 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Made with seedance 2.5

Prompt 👇 
Create a cute, heartwarming 9:16 vertical animated video featuring two adorable anthropomorphic baby bunnies sitting side by side on a cozy beige sofa in a warm, elegant living room.

CHARACTERS:
A fluffy cream-colored bunny wearing a simple blue T-shirt and a small gold necklace with a bunny-shaped pendant.
A fluffy pastel-pink bunny wearing a delicate gold necklace and a cute pink bow on her head.
Both bunnies have large expressive brown eyes, soft fluffy fur, tiny noses, chubby cheeks with a natural rosy blush, and charming childlike expressions.

SCENE & ACTION:
Start with both bunnies sitting on separate sofa cushions, facing each other and exchanging sweet, slightly shy glances. Their expressions gradually become playful and affectionate.

The cream bunny leans closer toward the pink bunny. The pink bunny also moves closer until their noses gently touch, followed by a sweet little kiss. They briefly close their eyes and smile affectionately.

Then show them becoming extra fluffy and excited, with their fur puffing up softly in a cute exaggerated animation. They remain close together, eyes closed, enjoying the affectionate moment.

End with both bunnies happily hugging each other tightly on the sofa, laughing with their eyes closed and mouths open in pure joy. Their little paws wrap around each other naturally, creating a warm, adorable friendship/romantic moment.

VISUAL STYLE:
Ultra-cute high-end 3D animated film style, soft realistic fluffy fur, detailed facial expressions, expressive eyes, smooth natural character animation, soft rounded anatomy, cinematic depth of field, warm cozy interior, gentle golden lighting, premium Pixar-like quality without copying any specific existing character.

CAMERA:
Vertical 9:16 composition. Medium two-shot at first, followed by a gentle slow push-in as they move closer. Use subtle camera movement and close-up framing during the kiss and final hug. Keep both characters centered and clearly visible.

LIGHTING & ATMOSPHERE:
Warm, soft indoor lighting with cozy beige and wooden tones. Elegant home interior in the background with a wooden cabinet, plants, and soft decor. Shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh, soft shadows, polished cinematic rendering.

MOTION:
Smooth, natural, playful animation with believable bunny movements, subtle ear movements, blinking, facial expressions, gentle body movement, and realistic fluffy fur physics. No sudden camera cuts.

MOOD:
Sweet, innocent, playful, adorable, affectionate, wholesome and heartwarming.

HIGH QUALITY:
Photorealistic fur detail combined with stylized 3D animation, cinematic composition, high detail, soft textures, clean rendering, natural motion, professional animated-film quality.
```

</details>

### Survival Run Through a Collapsing Kuala Lumpur

> When every second feels cinematic, the action never stops. Created with the power of Seedance 2.5 on @nemovideoai A high-intensity cinematic action sequence fea…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/a4fc7d20210a.jpg" width="600" alt="Survival Run Through a Collapsing Kuala Lumpur">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-single-page-premium-hollywood-disaster-action-storyboard-in-16-9-wide-7cc2f22eaa0c?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Xaroon_x | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Xaroon_x/status/2097267405399396457) | **公開日:** 2026-09-08 | **ヒート:** 77

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（58 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
TITLE: THE SURVIVAL RUN

Create a single-page, premium Hollywood disaster-action storyboard in 16:9 widescreen format with 12 cinematic panels covering one continuous 30-second survival sequence.

REFERENCE IMAGE 1 (Image1):
Use as the EXACT main character reference. Maintain the same facial identity, hairstyle, beard, body proportions, black shirt, black pants and overall appearance consistently in every panel. No character, face, hairstyle or clothing changes. Dust and debris may gradually appear as the disaster progresses.

REFERENCE IMAGE 2 (Image2):
Use as the EXACT storyboard design and layout reference. Match its premium cinematic panel arrangement, numbering, timecodes, black caption strips, typography hierarchy, borders and professional Hollywood storyboard presentation.

STYLE:
Photorealistic live-action Hollywood disaster film, ARRI Alexa 35, anamorphic cinematic lens, realistic Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia, practical stunt realism, believable destruction physics, handheld tracking, cinematic lighting, motion blur, dust, shattered glass, sparks and realistic debris.

CORE RULE:
ONE CONTINUOUS SURVIVAL SHOT. Every movement must logically connect. No teleportation or unexplained location changes. The same man constantly runs, stumbles, recovers, jumps, slides and changes direction while the collapsing city becomes the antagonist.

STORYBOARD PANELS:

1. 00:00–00:03 — EARTHQUAKE
Low-angle behind his feet as he runs through Kuala Lumpur. The ground shakes, buildings tremble, glass explodes and concrete crashes behind him. He looks back and accelerates.

2. 00:03–00:06 — COLLAPSE
Front tracking shot. A building façade collapses behind him. He changes direction and jumps over a metal barrier as debris destroys his original path.

3. 00:06–00:09 — CAR IMPACT
At an intersection, vehicles slide toward him. He dives at the last second, a car passes overhead, he rolls, recovers and keeps running.

4. 00:09–00:12 — ROAD CRACK
A huge fissure tears across the road. He runs onto a damaged car as a springboard and leaps across collapsing pavement.

5. 00:12–00:15 — FALLING GLASS
Side tracking shot. High-rise windows explode above him. Glass rains down as he shields his face and ducks beneath a falling sign.

6. 00:15–00:18 — COLLAPSE
A building collapses ahead. He spots a narrow gap, drops low and slides beneath falling debris, then emerges dusty and runs.

7. 00:18–00:21 — VEHICLE CHAOS
A bus crashes into cars. A spinning vehicle approaches. He jumps onto its hood, springs over the roof and lands on the opposite sidewalk.

8. 00:21–00:24 — ELEVATED ROAD FAILURE
An elevated road collapses ahead. He performs a desperate full-body gap jump, lands hard, rolls, recovers and runs.

9. 00:24–00:27 — DEBRIS CHASE
Heavy dust reduces visibility. Falling streetlights and concrete force sudden direction changes. He stumbles against a wall and pushes forward.

10. 00:27–00:30 — FINAL COLLAPSE
Camera faces him as he runs toward it. Massive buildings collapse behind him. He jumps a concrete barrier as a gigantic slab crashes down.

11. FINAL SHOT — STILL RUNNING
He emerges through a huge dust cloud, exhausted and covered in debris but clearly recognizable as Reference Image 1, still running toward camera.

12. CUT TO BLACK
Black screen with:
“THE SURVIVAL RUN”
“SOME DISASTERS YOU CAN’T OUTRUN”

CAMERA:
Low tracking, front pursuit, side tracking, shoulder-mounted chase, whip-pans and ground-level movement. Camera always follows his momentum. No drones, floating cameras or impossible rotations.
```

</details>

```
Multi-shot sequence with hard cuts between shots. Keep the same characters and visual style throughout. No music or score — only natural diegetic sound.\n\nSHOT 1 \nA dirty, exhausted 18-year-old girl with dark auburn-brown hair in a messy low ponytail, warm brown eyes, faded olive-green military jacket, backpack and gloves, aims a bolt-action rifle inside an abandoned ornate mansion. Sweat, dirt, a cheek scrape and tears cover her face. Her hands tremble. She closes her eyes as a tear rolls down, then hears something and lowers the rifle, visibly heartbroken.\n\nSHOT 2\nOver her shoulder, an infected golden-coated Golden Retriever sits on the debris-covered parquet floor. Matted fur, cloudy white eyes, wounds and dark veins. He trembles while softly whimpering and growling.\n\nSHOT 3 \nShe slowly crouches, places the rifle on the floor, wipes her tears and gives the dog a small broken smile. She quietly speaks to him, pulls a worn yellow-green tennis ball from her jacket and gently rolls it toward him.\n\nSHOT 4\nExtreme low-angle close-up of the tennis ball rolling through dust across the cracked parquet floor. It stops directly in front of the dog's paws. Hold on the still ball.\n\nHARD CUT / TRANSFORMATION\n\nSHOT 5\nSame low camera angle and ball position, but now on a clean sunny residential basketball court. A healthy golden-coated Golden Retriever suddenly grabs the ball. His eyes are clear and warm brown, fur clean, playful and energetic. He shakes and plays with the ball.\n\nSHOT 6\nWide shot of the basketball court. The woman throws the ball and the happy dog sprints after it, catches it, runs back and drops it at her feet, ready to play.\n\nSHOT 7\nClose-up of the clean, unharmed woman laughing while affectionately ruffling the dog's neck and chest. The dog happily pants beside her.\n\nSHOT 8\nWide peaceful shot. The woman and dog sit together in a pristine dark-green vintage convertible beside the court. She shares food from a tin can with him. He gently takes it and licks her fingers.\n\nCONSISTENCY\nSame woman throughout: dark auburn-brown hair, loose low ponytail, warm brown eyes, olive-green military jacket, khaki cargo pants and brown boots.\nSame Golden Retriever: infected and injured in the mansion, healthy and playful after the transformation.\n\nMansion: abandoned ornate interior, tall arched windows, torn dark-red curtains, peeling damask wallpaper, shattered gilt mirror, broken furniture, debris, ceiling hole and strong sunlight beams.\n\nCourt: clean residential basketball court, white lines, iron fence, flowering shrubs, stone townhouses, warm late-afternoon sunlight and soft shadows. Dark-green vintage convertible with chrome trim.\n\nVisual style: ultra-realistic, photorealistic, natural human and animal movement, handheld camera, shallow depth of field, ARRI Alexa 35 look, warm cinematic grade, subtle grain.\n\nAudio: ONLY natural breathing, house ambience, dog sounds, foots
```

Preserve the exact facial features, identity, skin tone, and natural facial proportions from @image1 throughout. Do not copy the reference hairstyle or outfit. Outfit: lightweight cream sleeveless blouse, high-waisted faded blue jeans, white canvas sneakers, small gold hoop earrings, loose natural wavy hair. Authentic Korean woman. Late-2000s personal vlog aesthetic. Extremely raw handheld flip-camera footage with heavy camera shake, spontaneous reframing, partial face crops, focus hunting, exposure shifts, warm faded colors, mild digital noise, and authentic home-video imperfections. No posing, no cinematic glamour, no stabilization, no modern color grading.

00:00–00:04 Walking quickly down a narrow seaside alley while holding the camera, suddenly spotting the ocean at the end of the street. She turns the camera toward it and excitedly says in Korean, "잠깐, 바다가 보여요!"

00:04–00:08 Runs toward a small harbor, laughing as the camera shakes heavily. She reaches the railing, leans over carefully, and watches fishing boats moving across the water.

00:08–00:12 Spots a fisherman pulling in a small fishing net and asks if she can help. She grabs one end of the net, struggles with the weight, laughs, and says in Korean, "생각보다 훨씬 무거운데요?"

00:12–00:16 The fisherman shows her a freshly caught fish. She reacts with wide-eyed surprise, steps backward laughing, then cautiously reaches out to touch it while saying, "우와, 진짜 크다!"

00:16–00:20 Walks into a tiny harbor-side snack shop, discovers a bowl of freshly made fish cake soup, sits at the counter, takes a sip, and smiles with satisfaction.

00:20–00:24 Steps outside holding the warm cup, notices a group of neighborhood grandmothers playing a casual Korean card game at a plastic table. She joins them briefly, laughs at their playful reactions, and gestures toward the camera.

00:24–00:27 Walks along the harbor promenade eating a small ice cream, filming boats and seagulls before accidentally getting a little ice cream on her nose. She notices it and laughs.

00:27–00:30 Stops beside the harbor railing as the wind moves her hair, turns the camera toward herself, smiles naturally, and says in Korean, "오늘 진짜 재밌었다. 다음에 또 올게요. 안녕!" She waves and the recording ends abruptly.

Natural ambience only: ocean waves, seagulls, fishing boats, harbor conversations, footsteps, wind, net ropes, shop sounds, card-table laughter, distant scooters, and neighborhood activity. No background music, subtitles, logos, or watermarks. The footage should feel exactly like a genuine late-2000s handheld flip-camera Korean vacation vlog with spontaneous actions, imperfect framing, authentic Korean dialogue, realistic human movement, and natural interactions. Aspect ratio: 16:9.
```

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（31 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic cinematic commercial for Nike Air Max, combining premium sports advertising, creativity, subtle comedy, and a surprising twist.

Keep the female creative director’s face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and appearance perfectly consistent throughout.

0–4s — THE DEADLINE
Late-night creative studio. A stylish young female creative director sits surrounded by monitors and unfinished projects. Nike Air Max sneakers sit on her desk. Notifications appear rapidly. She says:
“Five projects. One deadline.”

4–8s — THE IDEA
She looks at the Nike Air Max, gets an idea, rapidly creates a campaign on her computer, and hits ENTER. The screen flashes.

8–16s — THE CAMPAIGN
A powerful athlete wearing the same Nike Air Max runs through multiple cinematic environments: a futuristic city at sunrise, rain-soaked streets, and mountains. Show extreme close-ups of the sneakers, realistic Air Max cushioning compression, pavement impacts, water splashes, and seamless match cuts between locations.

16–21s — DONE
Back in the studio, her monitors display polished Nike Air Max campaign visuals. Only seconds have passed. Shocked, she says:
“Wait… that's it?”

21–26s — THE TWIST
She relaxes with coffee. A notification appears: CAMPAIGN APPROVED. She smiles. Another appears:
CLIENT: “Can we make 10 more?”
Her smile disappears. She looks at the camera and says:
“Never mind.”

26–30s — HERO REVEAL
Extreme cinematic product shot of Nike Air Max on a dark reflective surface. Dramatic lighting, floating particles, slow camera rotation. Final frame:
NIKE — JUST DO IT.

STYLE: Ultra-photorealistic, premium global sports commercial, cinematic lighting, realistic skin, fabric, reflections, shadows, natural motion, dynamic camera movement, shallow depth of field, subtle slow motion. No cartoon style, distorted anatomy, fake logos, fake text, watermarks, or altered shoe design.

AUDIO: Keyboard and notification sounds at the start, building into energetic cinematic music during the running sequence. Powerful footsteps, sneaker impacts, water splashes, and bass hits. Music drops for the comedy moment and ends with a strong cinematic hit.
```

Style: Cinematic live-action seamlessly blended with 2D hand-drawn line-art animation, using a playful rotoscoping-inspired transformation effect.

First-person POV throughout. A hand holding a blue pencil appears at the bottom of the frame, seemingly tracing over real-world objects. Wherever the pencil moves, the live-action environment gradually transforms into expressive 2D illustrations.

Scene Transitions & Subjects:

- Train: A white-and-green train travels across an elevated urban overpass. As the blue pencil traces along it, the train smoothly converts into clean black-and-white sketch line art, while retaining its movement and perspective.
- Cat: An orange tabby cat casually walks along a paved sidewalk. The pencil follows its movement, transforming the cat into a white hand-drawn cartoon with stylized illustrated shadows.

Overall Effect: The video should feel as though the blue pencil is literally drawing reality into an animated world, with each subject transforming organically rather than appearing as a sudden cut or visual effect.

```
Created a video, 30 second, featuring a futuristic white supercar driving through heavy traffic on a modern highway in Seoul during golden hour. The video begins with a wide aerial shot of multiple cars, buses, and trucks moving along a busy elevated road, with a beautiful city skyline in the background and warm sunlight reflecting off the vehicles. The camera slowly moves closer to a sleek, futuristic white sports car with sharp aerodynamic lines, a low-profile body, and an aggressive modern design. Inside the car, a beautiful young Korean woman with long, straight black hair is driving calmly, wearing an elegant cream-colored outfit. Capture her natural facial expressions, realistic skin texture, and focused eyes as she holds the steering wheel with confidence. Show cinematic close-up shots of her face through the side window and a reflection of her face in the rearview mirror, creating a luxurious automotive commercial atmosphere. The camera then cuts to a detailed rear view of the white supercar, highlighting its distinctive red LED taillight strip, sculpted rear bodywork, and futuristic exhaust design. Suddenly, the car accelerates powerfully, releasing thick white smoke from the rear tires as it performs a dramatic high-speed maneuver between the surrounding vehicles. Capture the tires spinning, smoke spreading across the highway, and nearby cars slowing down as the supercar surges forward. The camera follows the vehicle from behind and then switches to a wide cinematic side angle as the futuristic car launches into an impossible airborne leap above the highway, soaring over the river with the city skyline glowing in the background. Transition to a sophisticated glass-walled luxury café overlooking the river, where two elegantly dressed people sit across from each other at a table with coffee cups, reacting with surprise as the flying supercar passes outside the enormous windows. Show the white sports car soaring above the city buildings before revealing a rooftop helipad on a modern skyscraper. The car lands smoothly on the rooftop, creating a spectacular futuristic cinematic moment. End with a close-up of the vehicle parked against the panoramic city skyline, its scissor-style door opening upward as the female driver begins to step out. Maintain consistent car design, character identity, hairstyle, and clothing throughout every scene. Use photorealistic CGI, ultra-detailed automotive textures, realistic reflections, volumetric smoke, natural golden-hour lighting, smooth camera movements, dynamic drone shots, dramatic tracking shots, and premium luxury-car commercial cinematography. Use a cinematic 16:9 aspect ratio, 24 fps, realistic motion blur, high dynamic range, and seamless transitions between scenes. The overall mood should feel luxurious, futuristic, thrilling, and visually spectacular, with no unwanted text, subtitles, logos, watermarks, or character distortions.
```

### A Husky Meets Two Rabbits in Snowy Mountains at Sunset

> The Most Unexpected Friendship in the Snow! ❄️🐺🐰 Seedance 2.5 PROMPT ⬇️ Create an ultra-realistic cinematic video of a beautiful black-and-white Siberian Husk…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/0d95cc091f2c.jpg" width="600" alt="A Husky Meets Two Rabbits in Snowy Mountains at Sunset">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-the-most-unexpected-friendship-in-the-snow-fc70546ac916?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @RizwanAly07 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/RizwanAly07/status/2110654938472952253) | **公開日:** 2026-10-15 | **ヒート:** 75

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（41 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
The Most Unexpected Friendship in the Snow! ❄️🐺🐰

Seedance 2.5

PROMPT ⬇️ 
Create an ultra-realistic cinematic video of a beautiful black-and-white Siberian Husky with striking blue eyes exploring a snowy mountain landscape at sunset. The environment features pristine white snow, snow-covered pine trees, enormous snow-covered rocks, distant blue mountains, and warm golden sunlight illuminating the snow. Every animal must look completely realistic, with detailed fur, natural movements, and believable interactions.

SCENE 1 — THE HUSKY APPROACHES 
A beautiful black-and-white Siberian Husky walks slowly through deep, powdery snow. Its paws sink naturally into the snow, sending tiny snow particles into the air. The camera follows the dog from a low angle as it lowers its head and sniffs the ground curiously. Warm sunset light illuminates its fur.

SCENE 2 — SOMETHING MOVES 
The Husky suddenly notices movement near a large snow-covered rock. It stops, raises its head, and turns its blue eyes toward the rock. Its ears stand upright, and its nose twitches as it investigates. The camera smoothly moves closer to capture its curious expression.

SCENE 3 — THE SURPRISE 
A tiny, fluffy brown bunny cautiously peeks out from behind the rock. A second, smaller white bunny appears just behind it. Both rabbits have realistic fur, twitching noses, upright ears, and tiny paws. The Husky watches them with curiosity while maintaining a natural, relaxed posture.

SCENE 4 — THE ADORABLE MOMENT 
The brown bunny hops forward through the fresh snow, followed by the tiny white bunny. The Husky gently lowers its head toward them, sniffing the snow nearby. The rabbits pause and look toward the dog. Their contrasting sizes create a beautiful cinematic composition.

SCENE 5 — THE HEARTWARMING ENDING 
Both bunnies hop closer to the Husky. The dog remains calm and gently tilts its head, watching their movements with curious blue eyes. The camera slowly pulls back to reveal the three animals together in the glowing snowy landscape, surrounded by majestic mountains and warm sunset light.

🎥 Camera & Quality Instructions

Photorealistic animal fur, eyes, whiskers, paws, and facial expressions.

Natural animal anatomy and believable movement.

Smooth cinematic tracking shots with realistic depth of field.

Golden-hour lighting with cool blue shadows across the snow.

Realistic snow particles, pawprints, and subtle wind.

Natural ambient audio: soft winter wind, paws crunching in snow, and gentle rabbit movements.

No dialogue, text, subtitles, logos, or watermarks.

No cartoon appearance, artificial CGI look, distorted anatomy, duplicated animals, or sudden scene transitions.

Maintain consistent animal appearances throughout the entire video.
```

</details>

```
Made with seedance 2.5

Prompt 👇 
1. A cute chubby toddler boy, around 2–3 years old, with fair skin, huge expressive green eyes, short golden-blond hair with a small curled tuft on top, rosy cheeks, and a mischievous smile. He wears a bright yellow one-piece pajama covered with tiny orange duck patterns and a blue zipper.
2. His father, a friendly middle-aged man with neatly styled dark-brown hair, thick eyebrows, round black glasses, a large brown mustache, and a warm but slightly strict expression. He wears a light-blue shirt with a darker blue sweater vest and brown pants.

Environment:
A bright, playful kindergarten classroom filled with pastel colors. Rainbow decorations, colorful clouds, stars, alphabet blocks, children's tables and chairs, toys, teddy bears, large windows, and soft warm daylight. The entire environment should feel cheerful, whimsical, safe, and child-friendly.

Animation and story:
Start with the toddler standing confidently in the classroom with a mischievous expression while his father stands behind him. The father looks suspicious and points toward the child as if questioning him.

The toddler looks up at his father with an innocent expression and answers confidently. The father becomes more suspicious and asks whether the child has been eating sugar.

The toddler tries to deny it while smiling mischievously. Show a close-up of the toddler laughing with his eyes closed, revealing tiny teeth and a playful expression.

Cut back to the father and toddler together. The father raises one finger and questions him about telling lies.

Then the father leans closer toward the toddler and gestures toward the child's mouth, telling him to open his mouth.

End with an extreme close-up of the toddler laughing uncontrollably with eyes closed and a huge innocent smile while colorful confetti/star shapes appear around him.

Visual style:
High-end 3D animated children's movie quality, Pixar-inspired level of polish without copying any specific copyrighted character, soft rounded character design, oversized expressive eyes, adorable facial expressions, smooth skin shading, detailed clothing, vibrant pastel colors, soft global illumination, cinematic depth of field, volumetric lighting, glossy yet natural materials, beautiful subsurface scattering, clean character animation.

Camera:
Use dynamic cinematic camera movements with medium shots, close-ups, and expressive reaction shots. Smooth dolly-in movements, gentle camera transitions, shallow depth of field during close-ups, and stable framing. Keep the toddler's face highly expressive and clearly visible.

Facial animation:
Strong lip-sync, natural blinking, eyebrow movement, eye direction changes, cheek movement, smiling, laughing, surprised expressions, and exaggerated children's cartoon emotions.

Motion:
Smooth professional 3D character animation, natural hand gestures, believable body movement, subtle head movements, expressive pointing and reactions. Avoid stiff animation or unnatural movements.

Lighting:
Warm soft daylight coming through classroom windows, colorful ambient bounce light, soft shadows, subtle rim lighting, bright cheerful atmosphere.

Audio:
Cute child voice for the toddler, warm male father voice, playful children's background music, subtle classroom ambience, expressive comedic timing, clear dialogue and accurate lip synchronization.

Dialogue timing:
Father: “Johnny, Johnny!”
Toddler: “Yes, Papa?”
Father: “Eating sugar?”
Toddler: “No, Papa!”
Father: “Telling lies?”
Toddler: “No, Papa!”
Father: “Open your mouth!”
Toddler: [laughs]

Overall mood:
Funny, adorable, innocent, playful, highly engaging children's animation with exaggerated facial expressions and comedic timing.

Output:
Ultra-detailed 3D animation, 9:16 vertical format, 4K quality, smooth motion, cinematic composition, consistent character identity throughout the entire video, professional children's animation, polished rendering.
```

### A Korean Woman’s 30-Second Taiwan Travel Vlog

> Seedance 2.5 on @DomoAI_ Prompt AN EXCITING TAIWAN TRAVEL VLOG — 30 SEC MUSIC CUT SUBJECT: one fictional adult Korean woman in her early twenties, with large ro…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/4d344a6d9409.jpg" width="600" alt="A Korean Woman’s 30-Second Taiwan Travel Vlog">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-seedance-2-5-on-95624dd6486e?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2099847404266918037) | **公開日:** 2026-09-15 | **ヒート:** 75

**安定度:** 60/100

**再テスト:** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 60.2) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-seedance-2-5-on-95624dd6486e/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（46 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Seedance 2.5 on @DomoAI_ 

Prompt

AN EXCITING TAIWAN TRAVEL VLOG — 30 SEC MUSIC CUT

SUBJECT: one fictional adult Korean woman in her early twenties, with large round dark eyes, a small nose, coral-red lips, long side-parted black waves and small cubic-zirconia stud earrings. Keep the face established in the first shot consistent throughout. Visible pores and natural skin texture, without doll-like smoothness or a beauty filter.

OUTFIT: throughout all 30 seconds, she wears an indigo denim one-shoulder crop top and asymmetric-hem skirt, burgundy leather belt and pointed burgundy ankle boots. Keep the exposed shoulder, stitching and direction of the longer skirt panel consistent with their appearance in the first shot. Her outfit and hair do not change between locations.

CAMERA: the texture of a personally filmed phone travel video. She films the selfies. An off-screen travel companion films the two-handed chicken-cutlet handoff, her back in the market, and the restaurant food and eating shots. Changing operators does not introduce filming equipment or device UI into the image. Landscape 16:9, no black padding. Ordinary travel shots use a 26mm-equivalent phone wide angle, enough depth of field to read the surroundings, and natural iPhone colour. Handheld movement follows real footsteps; exposure and focus catch up slightly late. A fingerprint on the lens causes local light smearing beside strong light sources. No cinematic colour grade, studio lighting or added post-production glow. People in the surroundings move independently and do not stare into the lens for long.
EXCEPTIONS: only the three restaurant shots from 17 to 23 seconds use a DV-tape texture, light noise and slightly delayed focus. This deliberately differs from the travel footage; phone texture returns at 23 seconds. The food close-up at 19–21 seconds and the sunset focus change at 23–26.5 seconds follow their own shot-specific depth-of-field directions.

MUSIC: upbeat BGM carries the excitement and rhythm across the travel montage. Location sounds do not repeatedly interrupt the music. Only the gondola section has a one-beat drop. Dialogue is brief and relaxed, with audible breaths and complete word endings. Mandarin is limited to the opening and closing greetings, delivered as newly learned phrases. The ㅋㅋㅋ in the dialogue means laughter, not spoken letters.

PROPS: the bubble-tea clerk directly hands her a clear cup; the chicken-cutlet stall owner directly hands her the fried-chicken bag. In the xiaolongbao restaurant, the waiter sets the steamer with its lid on the table and she eats with chopsticks in her right hand and a spoon in her left. Food and containers are used only in their respective food scenes.

(0:00–0:02) — Guesthouse window, Taipei morning. She films a selfie from a low angle on her phone, smiling broadly. Tilt Up extends the frame above her face to the view through the window. Taipei 101's green-glass tower rises beyond low grey buildings, rooftop water tanks and air-con units. Exposure catches up with the bright sky. Dialogue: "你好~ 대만 왔다!" Transition: Hard cut on beat.

(0:02–0:04) — Main-road junction. At the crossing, she turns the phone into selfie position. A packed queue of helmeted scooter riders leaves the white stop line as the light turns green, passing behind her. Overcast daylight, Handheld Selfie and deep focus. Dialogue: "스쿠터 뭐야 미쳤어ㅋㅋㅋ" Transition: Fast cut on beat.

(0:04–0:06) — Bubble-tea counter. She points the phone in her left hand downward to film. Her right hand reaches out first. With arm and torso visible, the aproned clerk lowers the clear cup upright into it. After she supports its base and side, the clerk releases it. The sealed film lid faces up; black brown-sugar streaks line the cup. A wide straw rests on the counter. Insert, briefly Static. Transition: Snap cut.

(0:06–0:08) — Chicken-cutlet stall. The companion films from beside her, including both her hands and the owner. The large fried cutlet, seasoned with pepper and chilli, is already in a white paper bag. She extends both hands first. With arms and torso visible, the owner lowers the bag with its opening facing up. Only after her two hands support the bottom does the owner release it. Steam rises above the bag. Handheld Insert under a daylight awning. Transition: Cut at the end of the handoff.

(0:08–0:10) — Longshan Temple. She starts with a selfie and raises the phone. Tilt Up shifts the view from her face to incense smoke, red lanterns and the dragon-carved roof. The sky above the eaves is bright in natural daylight. Transition: Cut during the upward movement.

(0:10–0:12.5) — Jiufen stepped lane. She extends her arm high for a selfie and speaks. As she tilts the phone downward in a Tilt Down, her face leaves the frame and layers of red lanterns along the narrow stone stairway and wooden teahouse windows on both sides come into view. A misty afternoon; light fog veils the lower stairs. Dialogue: "여기 진짜 영화 속인데" Transition: Cut while looking down the stairway.

(0:12.5–0:15) — Dihua Street market. The companion films a waist-height Follow Shot from behind her. She raises the chicken-cutlet bag in one hand and takes a bite while walking through the passage. On either side of her indigo denim outfit, sacks of dried seafood, dried shiitake, tea tins, red envelopes and herbal-medicine drawers pass in sequence. Other people independently browse or walk by. Overcast afternoon light. Transition: Cut on forward movement.

(0:15–0:17) — Maokong gondola. In this scene she is not holding food or its bag. She films downward through the glass floor, holding the phone at knee height in one hand. Green tea fields drift beneath her feet and a cable shadow passes across them. Low Static. The music drops for one beat and returns. Transition: Snap cut.

(0:17–0:19) — Xiaolongbao restaurant, DV texture begins. She is already seated; the companion films a Static Medium from eye level across the table. The waiter sets a bamboo steamer upright on the table with its lid on, then lifts the lid. Six xiaolongbao on white paper, shredded ginger and black vinegar. She watches and speaks while the waiter performs the action. Dialogue: "이거 육즙 터진다며?" Transition: Cut to the open steamer.

(0:19–0:21) — Broth close-up. The companion films a downward-looking Macro from beside the table, with shallow depth of field. One xiaolongbao is already on the spoon in her left hand. She opens the skin slightly with the tips of the chopsticks in her right hand; broth flows into the spoon. Steam brushes past the lens. DV texture continues. Transition: Cut from the filled spoon.

(0:21–0:23) — First mouthful. The companion films a close Handheld shot from across the table. Begin immediately after her first mouthful of xiaolongbao. She lowers the spoon in her left hand and smiles toward the lens. White restaurant fluorescent light, DV texture continues. Dialogue: "미쳤다 진짜." Transition: Cut to sunset footage with phone texture.

(0:23–0:26.5) — Tamsui Fisherman's Wharf, signature. Phone texture returns. She walks beside the railing while filming an extended-arm selfie. The white Lover's Bridge and its reflection on the sea catch orange sunset light; her long waves and asymmetric denim hem move in the wind. Rack Focus: focus moves from the initially sharp bridge behind her to her face, leaving the bridge softly blurred. Actual sunset light, without added post-production glow. 60% slow motion. Transition: Cut when focus settles on her face.

(0:26.5–0:30) — Shifen railway, night. She films a selfie with the phone extended in her right hand. Her left hand releases the lower edge of a red sky lantern bearing brush calligraphy. The lit lantern leaves her fingertips and rises into the sky. Shop lights and other sky lanterns remain around her. She waves at the lens with her now-free left hand while speaking. Dialogue: "그럼 모두 再見~!" That left hand covers the lens, ending at 30 seconds. Natural night phone exposure.

CONSISTENCY: preserve the same face and single denim outfit. Contact between hands and props is visible, without smeared fingers. Do not add subtitles, graphic text, logos or watermarks to the footage. Writing that belongs to the location or props, including the lantern calligraphy, remains. No filming equipment is visible in frame.

FINALLY: skin and movement should look like real travel footage, not AI-generated imagery. Dialogue remains clear within natural breathing, without slurred syllables or clipped word endings. Expressions and gestures respond to the situation rather than posing like an advertising model. #DomoAI #DomoAICPP
```

</details>

### A Journey Through The Last Supper in Three Dimensions

> What if you could see what Da Vinci NEVER painted? I went INSIDE The Last Supper using Seedance 2.5 in @FishCreativeHQ Prompt👇 upload the painting image as fir…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/88808feeb427.jpg" width="600" alt="A Journey Through The Last Supper in Three Dimensions">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-what-if-you-could-see-what-da-vinci-never-painted-870128258984?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @abxxai | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/abxxai/status/2110709089710088537) | **公開日:** 2026-10-15 | **ヒート:** 74

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（87 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
What if you could see what Da Vinci NEVER painted?

I went INSIDE The Last Supper using Seedance 2.5 in @FishCreativeHQ

Prompt👇

upload the painting image as first frame and use this video prompt

[First-Frame.png] Create a 20-second vertical 9:16 image-to-video film, using the supplied image as the exact first frame and matching final frame. One continuous camera journey through the room, with macro detail, a gradual emotional reveal, an orbit behind the central figure and a seamless return.

Build the supplied scene as complete 3D geometry while preserving its Renaissance oil-painted appearance. The table stands in real space, the tablecloth drapes over its edges, the food and vessels sit on its surface, and every figure has a fully dimensional body beneath their robes. As the camera passes around them, reveal the sides and rear surfaces hidden from the opening viewpoint. Retain the original brushwork, warm earthy colours, soft daylight and expressive faces.

IDENTIFY AND PRESERVE THE FIGURES

The central figure sits at the middle of the table. He has long wavy brown hair, a full brown beard and downcast, calm eyes. He wears a deep red robe with a rich blue mantle draped across his left shoulder and lap. His right hand rests palm-down on the tablecloth at the left of the frame; his left hand lies open, palm-up, beside a small glass of red wine.

To his right (left of frame) sits a young apostle with long golden-brown hair, a green mantle over a red garment, his sorrowful gaze turned toward the centre. In front of him, a balding older man with a dark beard, wearing red and blue, leans in with a pointing gesture. A partly visible figure with curly hair sits at the far left edge.

To his left (right of frame) a man with thick black curly hair and a dark beard, in a blue robe, turns to speak with a grey-bearded elder in an olive-green robe at the right edge, who raises both hands in an open, questioning gesture.

Keep every pose, gesture, gaze direction and costume exactly as in the reference. No figure moves.

THE ROOM AND TABLE

A dark coffered wooden ceiling. A grey back wall with three tall windows opening onto a sunlit Tuscan landscape of blue hills, cypress trees, terracotta villas and a bell tower. Dark blue and gold damask panels on both side walls.

A long table covered in white linen with a visible grid of fold creases, hanging almost to the floor. On it: scattered bread rolls and crumbs, pewter plates, a central plate of fish, the small glass of red wine, a bowl of green olives, a plate of red and green grapes at the right, a blue-and-white decorated bowl with bread at the left, and a dark glazed ceramic jug at the far left. Wooden trestle legs and the hems of robes are visible below the cloth.

0–3 SECONDS — ENTER OVER THE BREAD

Begin on the exact supplied image for approximately half a second.

The camera then flies forward into the room and descends toward the bread roll resting on the tablecloth just left of centre. Pass macro-close above it.

Reveal the roll's rounded volume, cracked golden crust, flour-dusted ridges and the small crumbs scattered around it, each casting a tiny shadow on the linen. The nearest crust passes softly out of focus beneath the lens as the camera glides on.

The tablecloth has physical relief: woven threads, raised fold creases and shallow dips where objects press into it. The bread falls behind the moving camera while the central figure stays fixed farther into the room. This changing perspective establishes real depth immediately.

3–7 SECONDS — GLIDE ACROSS THE TABLE TO THE WINE

Travel low and smoothly across the linen toward the centre of the table, skimming past the rim of the fish plate. Reveal the plate's worn pewter edge, its curved depth and the painted fish resting inside.

Continue to the small glass of red wine. Curve partly around it at macro distance, revealing the thickness of the glass, the flat surface of the wine, and daylight from the windows refracting through it as a soft red glow on the cloth.

The glass crosses the near foreground, the open palm-up hand appears just beyond it, and the back wall shifts more slowly behind. Maintain forward momentum throughout.

7–10 SECONDS — RISE ALONG THE OPEN HAND AND MANTLE

Rise past the open hand, revealing the gentle curve of the fingers, the creases of the palm and its weight resting on the cloth.

Continue upward along the blue mantle where it falls across his lap and left shoulder. Travel close to the fabric without touching it. Show the painted texture, the deep blue folds as dimensional surfaces: raised crests catch the window light, undersides fall into shadow, and the red robe shows beneath the mantle's edge.

The blue fabric passes beside the lens as his face comes into view above.

10–13 SECONDS — REVEAL THE CENTRAL FACE

Curve inward and slightly upward toward his face.

Approach a close view of his lowered eyes, calm brow, nose and the soft strands of his beard and hair. Preserve the exact face from the reference. Reveal the three-dimensional shape of the cheekbones and brow, and the subtle painted texture of the skin, fully connected to his neck and shoulders.

Ease the camera's speed for approximately one second while continuing a gentle sideways drift. Let the stillness and quiet sorrow register. He does not move; the emotion comes from discovering the expression already present in the painting.

13–17 SECONDS — ORBIT BEHIND HIM TO THE WINDOW

Continue past the outside of his face, over his blue-draped left shoulder, and orbit smoothly behind him into the narrow space between his back and the central window.

Reveal the back of his long wavy hair, the rear folds of the blue mantle and red robe, and the thickness of his shoulders beneath the fabric. His shoulder passes close beside the lens as a soft foreground shape.

Turn gently to look past him through the central window: the sunlit Tuscan hills, cypresses and villas gain real depth, the far hills hazy and the near trees sharper. Then continue the arc so the view swings back over his other shoulder, revealing from behind the backs of the apostles' heads, the young apostle's green mantle, and the whole table spread out before them toward where the viewer began.

17–20 SECONDS — RETURN TO THE ORIGINAL COMPOSITION

Rise and retreat in one smooth arc over the table, passing above the grey-bearded elder's raised open hands at the right, then curving back onto the opening camera axis.

Reveal all figures again in their exact positions. The bread, plates, wine glass, olives, grapes, decorated bowl and ceramic jug settle back onto the table as in the supplied image. The coffered ceiling, three windows and damask walls realign with the frame.

Gently decelerate into the exact opening viewpoint, lens perspective, framing and lighting. End on a frame matching the supplied image so the next repetition flows directly into the opening movement.

CAMERA, STYLE AND CONTINUITY

Use one uninterrupted physical camera path: over the bread, across the table to the wine, up the open hand and blue mantle, to the central face, around behind him to the window, back over his shoulder and out to the original wide composition.

Use natural motion blur and shallow depth of field during macro passes. Transfer focus smoothly to the next detail along the route. Foreground objects pass the lens because the camera moves around them; no figure changes pose, blinks, speaks or moves toward the camera.

Maintain one coherent room with stable geometry and consistent warm daylight from the windows. Hidden surfaces belong to the existing figures, clothing, table and walls and match their visible design. Preserve the oil painting's brushwork, canvas texture and colour at every scale.

No cuts, crash zooms, dissolves, flat canvas flyovers, wall-relief effects, scene morphing, additional people, new props, extra food, text or watermark.
```

</details>

```
Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9, 24fps hyper-realistic Korean live-action action thriller with dry dark comedy. Nighttime outside a modern Korean convenience store, wet pavement reflecting storefront lights.

PYONA exits carrying a small paper bag. Three intimidating adult men surround her. The leader says, “You have three choices: give us your phone, apologize, or fight.”

PYONA calmly studies the gap between them and says, “I'll take number four.” She walks through the opening and adds, “You leave.” The leader's smile disappears.

He grabs her forearm. She looks at his hand and says, “That was the wrong answer.” When he pulls her, she steps toward him, rotates with his force and redirects his momentum into the convenience-store door.

A second man charges with a punch. PYONA sidesteps and redirects him into the first man. A third attacks from behind; she steps aside and redirects him into stacked plastic crates, which collapse from the collision.

The three men freeze. The leader asks, “Who are you?” PYONA calmly replies, “I already told you.” She picks up her bag, walks past them, then looks back and says, “Number four.”

Cut to black.

Use grounded physical choreography: realistic weight, momentum, inertia, friction, leverage, balance and collision reactions. PYONA wins through timing and redirection, never superhuman strength.

Controlled cinematic camera with consistent screen direction and clear full-body framing during physical actions. Natural nighttime ambience and realistic impact sounds only. No music or narration.

Negative prompt: supernatural strength, flying bodies, wire-fu, invisible force, impossible throws, premature falls, teleportation, duplicate characters, appearance drift, costume changes, location changes, excessive camera shake, exaggerated slow motion, cartoon physics, blood, gore, weapons, broken bones, head impacts, logos, watermark.

She abruptly turns toward a nearby colleague and charges, forcing him behind a glass partition.

She repeatedly strikes the glass while staring directly at him, her completely black eyes and pulsing dark veins clearly visible. Two more scientists enter and freeze when she suddenly becomes perfectly still, then slowly rotates her head toward them. Around the laboratory, additional containment chambers begin cracking as warning lights activate. Another infected creature breaks free, sending the scientists scrambling for emergency equipment. They use a powerful emergency strobe and fire extinguisher to disorient the creatures before pushing a heavy steel cart across the corridor and retreating behind a security door.

The surviving scientists reach an emergency corridor and catch their breath as red warning lights wash over their exhausted faces. The sealed laboratory door violently shakes behind them. The camera gradually approaches its small observation window, revealing the infected woman standing motionless in the darkness, still wearing her white lab coat and blue jeans. Her pale face, prominent dark veins, and completely black eyes are visible through the glass. She slowly tilts her head toward the camera as the emergency lights flicker across her face. Cut to black.

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（63 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
FORMAT: 30 seconds | 16:9 | Ultra-photorealistic live-action | Continuous handheld vacation vlog

Preserve the exact face, hairstyle, identity, skin tone, facial features, and body proportions from <<<image_1>>> throughout the entire video. The same woman must appear consistently in every shot.

VISUAL STYLE: Authentic late-2000s personal vacation vlog recorded on a cheap handheld flip camera. Extremely raw and imperfect footage. Heavy natural camera shake, awkward handheld framing, occasional partial face crops, sudden reframing, autofocus hunting, exposure fluctuations, blown highlights, slightly soft image quality, warm faded colors, mild digital noise, compression artifacts, and subtle motion blur. No cinematic camera movements, no gimbal stabilization, no polished composition, no modern color grading, no beauty filter, no artificial sharpness.

Outfit: oversized grey hooded sweatshirt, black leggings, worn white running shoes, hair pulled back into a low messy ponytail, small silver stud earrings, a canvas tote bag slung over one shoulder. The entire video must feel like one genuine recording captured by a friend on an early-morning trip through a fishing village in Sokcho, South Korea, not a staged commercial or cinematic recreation.

00:00–00:04 — EARLY MORNING ARRIVAL
The woman walks along a quiet harbor path as the sun rises, fishing boats bobbing gently in the water behind her, seagulls circling overhead.
The camera shakes slightly as it follows her from the side.
She says:
"It's so early, but I really wanted to see the fish market before it gets busy."

00:04–00:08 — THE FISH MARKET
She steps into a covered harbor market filled with tanks of live fish, crabs, and squid, fishermen unloading crates from small boats nearby.
The camera zooms unsteadily on a tank of wriggling crabs, focus hunting before snapping back to her face.
She reacts with a mix of fascination and mild alarm.

00:08–00:12 — TALKING WITH A FISHERMAN
An older fisherman hoists a large fish onto a table for her to see up close.
She leans in curiously, asks a question, and he answers while gesturing with his hands.
She laughs and says to the camera:
"He says this one was caught just an hour ago!"

00:12–00:16 — TRYING RAW SQUID
A nearby stall vendor offers her a small plate of freshly sliced raw squid with dipping sauce.
She hesitates for a second, then takes a bite, chewing slowly with a surprised expression.
She turns to the camera and says:
"Okay wow, that's way fresher than I expected."

00:16–00:20 — HELPING SORT THE CATCH
A fisherwoman nearby is sorting fish into baskets and waves her over to help.
She kneels down and awkwardly tries to sort a few fish, nearly dropping one, laughing at herself.
The fisherwoman laughs too and pats her on the back encouragingly.

00:20–00:24 — WALKING THE HARBOR
She walks along the docks past rows of moored fishing boats, ropes coiled on the ground, nets drying in the morning sun.
A couple of fishermen nod and greet her; she waves back cheerfully.
The camera briefly swings toward the boats before swinging back to her.

00:24–00:27 — QUIET MOMENT
She stops at the edge of the harbor, looking out over the calm water as the sun fully rises over the horizon.
For a moment there's no dialogue — just waves lapping, distant gulls, and the creak of boats.
She smiles softly and says:
"Mornings like this make the early wake-up worth it."

00:27–00:30 — NATURAL ENDING
She turns back toward the camera, gives a small wave, and says:
"Okay, harbor adventure done. See you next time!"
She laughs, turns, and walks off along the dock.
The camera lingers a moment before the footage naturally ends with a slight shake and imperfect cut.

AUDIO
Natural location sound only:
- waves lapping against the dock
- seagulls calling
- boat engines idling in the distance
- fishermen calling out to each other
- crates being unloaded
- water splashing in fish tanks
- footsteps on wet pavement
- light morning breeze

Sometimes the smallest moments become the most magical adventures. 🌧️🚂✨

Created on seedance 2.5

Prompt
Create a 15-second cinematic fantasy slice-of-life sequence following a young boy waiting alone at a quiet rural railway station on a rainy evening. Begin with a wide establishing shot across multiple wet railway tracks, slowly revealing a small countryside station surrounded by endless green fields, distant mountains, utility poles, and a dark cloudy sky → reveal the young boy standing beneath the station shelter, wearing a bright yellow raincoat, white shorts, and vivid red rain boots, quietly waiting for his train as heavy rain falls around him → transition to a low-angle cinematic close-up of his red rain boots standing beside a shallow rain puddle, with the warm reflection of a small station lantern shimmering across the wet ground → cut to a side profile shot of the boy sitting alone on a wooden railway bench, looking quietly toward the empty tracks while rain falls in the background → transition to a mysterious close-up beneath the wooden bench as a tiny strange green creature wearing an oversized pointed green hat slowly peeks out from the shadows, watching the boy with large curious eyes → cut to a cinematic close-up of the boy's face as he notices the mysterious creature, his expression changing naturally from surprise to gentle curiosity and a warm smile → transition to a detailed close-up of the boy opening a small cloth lunch bag and taking out a piece of bread while sitting on the bench → cut to the tiny green creature watching the food from beneath the bench, cautiously moving closer with innocent curiosity → transition to a medium shot as the boy gently places a piece of bread on the bench for the little creature, creating a quiet emotional connection between them → cut to a wide cinematic shot of the empty railway tracks stretching into the rainy countryside as a distant train headlight slowly appears on the horizon → transition into a dramatic forward tracking shot along the wet railway tracks, the approaching train becoming brighter as rain continues falling and its warm headlights reflect across the rails → cut back to the station as the train arrives and stops beside the platform, its warm interior lights glowing against the cold blue rainy atmosphere → final shot becomes a cinematic medium-wide shot of the young boy stepping into the warmly illuminated train while briefly looking back toward the tiny green creature near the bench, as rain continues falling outside and the train prepares to leave the quiet countryside station
```

</details>

### POV Cliff Jump into a Turquoise Volcanic Canyon Spring

> omg...Seedance 2.5 can do real POV physics now one prompt on @FishCreativeHQ.. bookmark this 👇 Prompt: SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/ba78b17e3025.jpg" width="600" alt="POV Cliff Jump into a Turquoise Volcanic Canyon Spring">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-a-hidden-volcanic-spring-canyon-deep-in-a-pacific-northwest-pine-forest-on-a-br-ff48a4636c95?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @abxxai | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/abxxai/status/2103861170625286334) | **公開日:** 2026-09-26 | **ヒート:** 73

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（50 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | 22s | 1080x1920 | 9:16 | 30fps | 1080p

SCENE AND LOCATION
A hidden volcanic spring canyon deep in a Pacific Northwest pine forest on a bright summer day. A narrow, deep gorge with steep, very high walls of dark gray basalt made of vertical hexagonal columns, streaked with tan and rust tones and covered in patches of bright green moss, ferns and small shrubs.
At the bottom is a crystal-clear natural spring pool. The shallow edges glow bright turquoise and aqua, fading to deep sapphire blue in the center. The water is so transparent that the bottom is fully visible: honeycomb-patterned volcanic rock, mossy boulders, and two long pale submerged logs lying diagonally across the bottom.
Two white waterfalls pour into the pool: a thin one at the far back of the canyon and a larger one spilling from the right-hand cliff wall, making white foam where it hits the water. Tall dark-green conifer and pine trees line the canyon rim. Above is a vivid clear blue sky with a few small white clouds. Hard, bright midday sunlight with strong contrast, and sun sparkles on the water.

THE JUMPER
A young man seen only from his own first-person view. Tanned bare feet and lightly hairy shins, and loose navy-blue swim shorts with a large mustard-yellow hibiscus flower pattern and white drawstrings. No shirt, no shoes, no gloves. His hands and forearms appear naturally in frame when he moves them.

CAMERA AND REALISM
First-person POV from a GoPro strapped to his forehead. It looks like real action-cam footage, not a cinematic camera. 16mm-equivalent ultra-wide lens with slight barrel distortion, and saturated but natural color.
Use real action-cam behavior throughout: small head bob and micro-jitter from breathing and body weight, rolling-shutter wobble on fast moves, quick auto-exposure shifts between bright sky and the shaded canyon, a small sun flare on upward tilts, light sensor noise in the shadows, and wind buffeting that shakes the frame during the fall.
The cliff is about 35 meters high. Gravity, body motion and water physics are fully realistic.

0-2s | THE EDGE (real time)
POV looking straight down from the top of the basalt cliff. His bare feet stand on the rough, dark rock ledge at the bottom of frame, with the shorts visible at the lower edge. Both hands hover forward at the sides of frame. The turquoise pool, the submerged logs and the waterfalls are far, far below. His toes curl over the edge and small grit crumbles off. His right hand tosses a pink-and-white striped inflatable tube out over the drop; it tumbles down and lands as a tiny speck on the water far below. Wind, distant waterfall roar, upbeat chill electronic music starts.

2-4s | THE COUNTDOWN (real time)
A quick nervous tilt down at the drop, a sharp exhale, then two fast steps back from the edge, with the ledge bouncing in frame. Young male voice, hyped: "Three, two, one!"

4-7s | THE TAKEOFF (natural slow motion, about 50% speed)
Two running steps forward, then his last foot pushes off the ledge in slow motion. Rock grit kicks up and floats in the sunlight, the cliff edge slides away beneath his feet, his arms swing forward and out wide, and the whole turquoise canyon opens up below. The slow motion looks like a real high-frame-rate action-cam clip, not a frozen hang. Deep bass swell, stretched wind.

7-13s | THE FALL (real time, fast and continuous)
The speed ramps smoothly back to normal and the stomach drops.
7-9s: The frame dips and wobbles, his arms flail slightly for balance at the edges of frame, and his feet kick at the bottom of frame. A loud yell: "Woooooo!"
9-11s: The basalt column wall, moss and ferns streak upward past the lens, and the right-side waterfall rushes by with spray catching the sun. Wind roars louder and the frame shakes from air buffeting.
11-13s: The glowing turquoise water rushes up and fills the frame, and the pink tube and submerged logs grow fast. His arms snap tight to his body and his legs lock straight for a feet-first pencil dive. The music builds to the drop.

13-14s | IMPACT
A hard feet-first hit into the water and a white explosion of bubbles swallows the lens. Loud plunge splash, deep impact whoosh, and the music goes heavily muffled underwater.

14-17s | UNDERWATER (short, energetic, real time)
A violent swirl of silver bubbles clears to reveal crystal-clear blue water, bright sun shafts cutting down from the surface, the pale submerged logs and honeycomb volcanic rock bottom below, and small fish darting away. His hands push into frame for one strong breaststroke pull, then fast kicks straight up toward the bright glowing surface, with bubbles streaming past the lens. No slow sinking.

17-19s | SURFACE BREAK
The camera bursts through the surface, water droplets sliding across the lens, and bright sunlight floods in. The music snaps back to full clarity on the beat drop. His hand grabs the pink-and-white tube floating right beside him.

19-22s | THE REWARD
POV bobbing on the turquoise water holding the tube, then a sharp tilt up revealing the towering basalt cliff, the tiny ledge he jumped from far above, the pine trees on the rim and the vivid blue sky, with the waterfall splashing on the right. Same voice, out of breath and triumphant: "Yeah, dude! That was insane!" Faint distant cheering. Music plays clean to the final frame.

AUDIO
Continuous upbeat chill electronic track: a bass swell on takeoff, a fast build through the fall, muffled underwater, then a clean drop on surfacing. Real ambient layers: wind that grows with speed, fabric flapping, heavy breathing, waterfall roar, splash, bubbles. Dialogue clear over the music.

KEEP
One continuous take, first-person only. The same shorts, feet and hands throughout, and the same canyon layout with two waterfalls, turquoise pool and submerged logs. The slow motion lasts only 3 seconds at takeoff. The fall is fast, real time and builds speed without pausing. Every beat is quick and punchy.

AVOID
Extra slow motion beyond the takeoff, hovering or floating mid-air, smooth cinematic gimbal movement, cuts to third person, showing the jumper's face, extra people, distorted hands or extra fingers, morphing cliffs, changing water color, text, subtitles, watermarks.
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（52 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
CHARACTER:
A young woman wearing a cute, stylish amusement-park outfit with a clearly visible Mickey Mouse ears headband. Keep the headband consistent throughout the entire video. She is excited, playful, and expressive.
A cheerful Turkish ice cream vendor stands behind a traditional Turkish ice cream stall, holding a long metal ice cream paddle.

0–3 SEC — ORDER
Start with a cinematic wide shot of the colorful amusement park, then smoothly move toward the Turkish ice cream stall.

The girl approaches the counter, smiling excitedly. Her Mickey Mouse ears headband is clearly visible. She points toward the ice cream.
The vendor prepares a cone with a scoop of ice cream and extends it toward her.

3–6 SEC — THE FIRST TEASE
Cut to a medium shot.

The girl eagerly reaches for the cone.

Just as she is about to grab it, the vendor smoothly pulls the ice cream away with the long paddle.
She looks at him in surprise.

He smiles mischievously and offers it again.

6–9 SEC — MORE TEASING
She reaches again.

The vendor smoothly moves the cone from left to right, then slightly upward, always just out of reach.

The girl follows the cone, laughing and becoming increasingly determined.

Keep the interaction fluid, realistic, and playful, with natural body movement and timing.
9–11 SEC — THE NOSE TRICK
The vendor finally brings the ice cream toward her.

She confidently leans forward, expecting to receive it.

Instead, he gently places a small dab of ice cream on the tip of her nose.

Close-up on her shocked expression.

She pauses, looks cross-eyed toward the ice cream on her nose, then looks back at the vendor with a playful annoyed expression.
11–15 SEC — SHE WINS
Before the vendor can tease her again, she quickly grabs the cone from the paddle.

She successfully gets the ice cream.

The vendor laughs and raises his hands in playful defeat.

She proudly holds up the cone, smiles toward the camera, wipes the ice cream from her nose, and takes a happy bite.

CINEMATOGRAPHY:
Photorealistic live-action, premium cinematic quality, warm golden-hour lighting, natural skin texture, realistic facial expressions, shallow depth of field, soft background bokeh, subtle anamorphic lens characteristics, natural motion blur, smooth gimbal movement, cinematic push-ins and tracking shots, realistic amusement-park atmosphere.

IMPORTANT:
Keep the Mickey Mouse ears headband clearly visible and consistent throughout the scene. Maintain consistent faces, wardrobe, hairstyle, ice cream cone, vendor paddle, and environment. All movements must be smooth and physically realistic. No exaggerated cartoon movement, no 3D animation, no jerky motion, no teleporting, no object duplication, and no unnatural hand movements.
```

</details>

### Crimson-Powered Armored Hero Battles Masked Men on a Rainy Night

> Pushing AI filmmaking to the next level with cinematic visuals, seamless transformations, and action-packed storytelling. #Loovaai Powered by Seedance 2.5 on @L…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/0dc41c20a4a2.jpg" width="600" alt="Crimson-Powered Armored Hero Battles Masked Men on a Rainy Night">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-crimson-flashpoint-48-seconds-16-9-12-shots-4-seconds-eaef000addde?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Xaroon_x | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Xaroon_x/status/2110248837432439129) | **公開日:** 2026-10-14 | **ヒート:** 72

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（37 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
CRIMSON FLASHPOINT — 48 seconds, 16:9, 12 shots × 4 seconds. Photorealistic Hollywood superhero action film, ARRI ALEXA 65, anamorphic look, subtle film grain.

LOCATION: Rain-soaked neon downtown intersection at night, glass skyscrapers, abstract cyan/magenta/amber signs, reflective wet asphalt, steam grates, parked cars, dark alley on the right. Heavy rain throughout; crimson is the dominant accent.

HERO (@image1): Preserve identical face, hair, beard, skin tone, age, and build in every shot. Original outfit before transformation; afterward, matte-black articulated armor with glowing crimson seams across ribs, arms, legs, and spine, segmented chest plate with a faint red core, fingerless armored gloves. No helmet or cape; face fully visible.

ENEMIES: Exactly three masked men in black tactical jackets, cargo pants, grey gloves, and matte-black full-face masks. Man 1 is tall and lean with a steel baton; Man 2 is broad with a crowbar; Man 3 has a medium build and baton. No dialogue.

SHOT 01 (00–04): Extreme-wide low-angle skyline under storm clouds. A bright crimson streak cuts diagonally through the sky, illuminating rain and glass towers.

SHOT 02 (04–08): Fast overhead tracking follows the falling streak toward street level. A faint human silhouette appears inside the light as crimson reflections sweep across the road.

SHOT 03 (08–12): Hero lands in a one-knee, one-fist crouch in his original outfit. Water explodes outward; red energy threads crawl across his arms and torso.

SHOT 04 (12–16): Smooth 360° orbit as crimson energy assembles the black armor panel by panel. The chest plate locks into place and red seams ignite.

SHOT 05 (16–20): 85mm close-up. Rain beads on his face and armor; a red spark flickers in his eyes as he looks forward with controlled determination.

SHOT 06 (20–24): Low side-tracking shot. Fully armored hero sprints down the wet street at superhuman speed, spraying water and leaving a thin crimson trail.

SHOT 07 (24–28): Over-the-shoulder shot. Three masked men emerge from the right-side alley, spreading out with weapons ready. Hero skids to a defensive stop.

SHOT 08 (28–32): Hero dashes forward as a crimson blur and strikes Man 1, launching him backward. Rain and water burst outward in slow motion.

SHOT 09 (32–36): Man 2 swings his crowbar. Hero ducks, spins, and lands one strike, sending him sliding through a puddle toward a parked car.

SHOT 10 (36–40): Man 3 lunges with his baton. Hero dodges inches away and delivers a powerful palm strike, blasting him backward through an explosive ring of water. Bright crimson flash at impact.

SHOT 11 (40–44): Wide low-angle pull-back. Hero stands calmly between all three fallen attackers as rain pours over his armor and crimson seams gradually dim.

SHOT 12 (44–48): Extreme close-up of the hero lifting his head with quiet confidence. A tiny red spark crosses his eyes before the image cuts to black.

AUDIO: Original cinematic orchestral-synth score, heavy rain, electrical crackle, realistic impacts, water splashes, accelerating percussion, and a powerful musical peak at Shot 10. No dialogue.

CONTINUITY: Maintain exact hero identity, armor design, enemy count, location layout, lighting, and screen direction across all shots. Realistic physics, consistent rain, natural motion blur, controlled speed ramps, clean cuts, and stable camera movement.

NEGATIVE: Cartoon, artificial CGI appearance, plastic skin, face changes, inconsistent armor, extra characters, duplicate bodies, extra fingers, deformed hands, cape, helmet, yellow accents, lightning-bolt emblem, blood, gore, impossible physics, random camera movement, jitter, flicker, readable text, logos, or watermark.
```

```
Shot 1 (0–1.2s): Character A, matching reference face/outfit, sits beside a passenger on a dim train, feverish, sweating, staring strangely at him.

Shot 2 (1.2–2.4s): Close-up: her trembling hand, faint dark veins appearing, shallow unnatural breathing.

Shot 3 (2.4–3.6s): Her head tilts unnaturally toward him, glassy unfocused eyes, tense profile close-up.

Shot 4 (3.6–4.8s): She suddenly lunges and bites his forearm, teeth breaking skin, his face frozen in shock.

Shot 5 (4.8–6s): Close-up of the wound as dark veins rapidly branch outward, skin blackening.

Shot 6 (6–7.2s): Veins race toward his elbow; skin pales, sweat beads on his forehead.

Shot 7 (7.2–8.5s): His eyes cloud milky white, pupils dilate, breathing becomes wet and ragged.

Shot 8 (8.5–9.7s): His body stiffens and twitches; black veins spread across his neck and jaw.

Shot 9 (9.7–11s): He throws his head back and screams inhumanly, face covered in black veins, slow motion.

Shot 10 (11–12.2s): His head snaps forward; bloodshot empty eyes and slightly unhinged jaw reveal the completed transformation.

Shot 11 (12.2–13.5s): He instantly lunges at another passenger and bites them, sudden violent impact.

Shot 12 (13.5–14.8s): Wider shot: nearby passengers turn, confusion becoming horror.

Shot 13 (14.8–16s): The bitten passenger stumbles back, clutching the wound as dark veins spread up the arm.

Shot 14 (16–17.3s): Passengers rise in panic, grabbing bags and backing away, wide cinematic shot.

Shot 15 (17.3–18.5s): The new victim convulses violently in the aisle as passengers recoil; overhead lights flicker.

Shot 16 (18.5–19.7s): Eyes roll back, then open bloodshot and empty; jaw twitches—the second transformation complete.

Shot 17 (19.7–21s): He lunges at the nearest passenger; screams erupt as the car descends into chaos, handheld camera.

Shot 18 (21–22.5s): Passengers scramble over seats and luggage toward the exit, bags spilling everywhere.

Shot 19 (22.5–24s): A group rushes toward the connecting door, looking back as infection spreads behind them.

Shot 20 (24–25.5s): Door slams shut; survivors barricade it with luggage and cushions as infected hands pound from behind.

Shot 21 (25.5–27.5s): Close-up through the glass: infected faces press against it as cracks spread, emergency lights pulsing.

Shot 22 (27.5–30s): Survivors huddle against the far wall, breathing hard and staring at the shaking door as train lights streak outside, cinematic outbreak ending.
```

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（80 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic Hollywood-level cinematic thriller scene titled “THE LAST CALL.” The entire sequence must feel like a scene from a high-budget live-action feature film, with realistic human acting, natural facial expressions, physically accurate movement, realistic rain, practical lighting, believable vehicle physics, detailed environments, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, natural skin texture, realistic reflections, and professional Hollywood cinematography. No anime, no CGI-looking visuals, no fantasy aesthetic, no cyberpunk, no neon lighting, no exaggerated visual effects.
MAIN CHARACTER: A realistic young woman in her late 20s, natural appearance, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair slightly damp from the humid weather, wearing a simple dark jacket. Maintain the exact same woman, face, hairstyle, clothing, car, weather and environment consistently throughout the entire sequence.

SETTING: A remote mountain road at night during heavy but realistic rainfall. Dark forested mountains surround the narrow winding road. Wet asphalt reflects only natural headlights and road markings. The atmosphere is tense, isolated and believable.

0–5 SECONDS

Interior of a moving car at night. Medium shot from the passenger side showing the woman driving alone. Rain continuously hits the windshield and realistic windshield wipers move back and forth. Dashboard illumination softly lights her face. She looks tired and emotionally distant while concentrating on the road. Camera slowly pushes toward her face. Natural breathing, subtle blinking and realistic micro-expressions.

5–10 SECONDS

Her smartphone suddenly begins ringing in the center console. Cut to an extremely realistic close-up of the phone screen.

Caller ID: “MOM”

The woman freezes.

Her eyes widen slightly. Her hands tighten around the steering wheel. She looks at the phone in disbelief. She hesitates for a moment, then reaches down and answers the call.

10–17 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her face as she listens.

A faint, emotional female voice comes through the phone:

“Don't take the next turn.”

The woman's expression changes from confusion to fear.

The call suddenly disconnects.

She looks at the phone.

The screen reads: CALL ENDED

Outside the windshield, a sharp mountain turn approaches through the heavy rain.

17–23 SECONDS

She immediately slows the car.

Camera moves to a forward-facing perspective through the windshield. The road curves sharply ahead.

Suddenly, distant headlights appear through the rain around the corner.

The woman looks toward them.

A large truck suddenly loses control and crashes through the roadside barrier at the exact location where her car would have been if she had continued driving.

The crash must look physically realistic and grounded, with believable vehicle momentum, rain, debris and impact. No exaggerated explosions.

23–27 SECONDS

Silence after the crash.

The woman sits completely still, breathing heavily, visibly shaken.

She slowly looks down at her phone.

The phone screen now shows:

NO SIM — NO SIGNAL

She looks confused and terrified.

27–30 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her eyes filling with tears.

She looks toward the empty passenger seat.

A subtle emotional pause.

In a trembling whisper, she says:

“Mom?”

Cut instantly to black.

FINAL AUDIO: Heavy rain, windshield wipers, realistic car interior ambience, distant thunder, phone ringing, muffled female voice, sudden silence after the crash, then only the woman's breathing.
```

One door. Five worlds. Endless possibilities. Where would you go? ✨🌍

made with Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI .
 
Here is the prompt🔽🔽🔽

Create a 28-second ultra-cinematic surreal fashion adventure starring my female AI model. Preserve her EXACT facial identity, facial structure, hairstyle, skin tone, body proportions and recognizable appearance throughout the entire video.

The film must feel like ONE continuous impossible journey, not separate AI-generated clips. Photorealistic, mysterious, premium and visually spectacular.

0–4 SEC — THE MYSTERIOUS HOTEL

Begin with an extreme cinematic close-up of my model's eyes.

Camera smoothly pulls backward, revealing her standing inside an impossibly long, luxurious old-European hotel corridor at night.

Warm golden wall lamps illuminate hundreds of identical doors stretching into the distance.

She wears an elegant flowing black evening dress with subtle jewellery.

She looks toward one particular door and confidently approaches it.

CAMERA: Slow dolly backward → smooth over-the-shoulder orbit as her hand reaches toward the brass handle.

She opens the door.

Intense white light floods through it and completely fills the lens.

SEAMLESS TRANSITION THROUGH THE LIGHT.

4–9 SEC — STORMY ICELAND

The camera passes through the doorway directly behind her.

Without stopping her walking motion, she emerges onto a spectacular Icelandic black-sand beach.

Towering dark cliffs, crashing ocean waves, mist and light rain surround her.

Strong wind naturally moves her hair and black dress.

CAMERA: Begin low beside her feet → rapidly rise into a cinematic tracking orbit around her.

Standing completely alone on the beach is another mysterious hotel door.

She reaches it and immediately opens it.

Camera follows directly through.

9–14 SEC — FUTURISTIC TOKYO

She steps through the same doorway directly into a futuristic Tokyo street at midnight.

Her walking movement continues seamlessly.

Wet streets reflect enormous neon signs, futuristic architecture and passing autonomous vehicles.

Crowds naturally move around her while she confidently walks toward camera.

CAMERA: 35mm anamorphic backward tracking shot → subtle handheld micro-movement → gentle push toward her face.

A sleek futuristic motorcycle suddenly races extremely close across the foreground.

For a fraction of a second, the motorcycle completely covers the camera.

USE THE MOTORCYCLE AS THE TRANSITION.

14–19 SEC — UNDERWATER BALLROOM

As the motorcycle clears the lens, she is suddenly walking through an enormous abandoned luxury ballroom deep underwater.

She does NOT wear diving equipment.

She walks normally while her dress and hair gently react to the surrounding water.

Gigantic floor-to-ceiling windows reveal the deep ocean outside.

A huge whale silently glides past the windows behind her.

Blue sunlight beams penetrate through the water while thousands of tiny particles float naturally through the scene.

CAMERA: Slow cinematic 180° orbit around her as she turns slightly toward the whale.

Suddenly hundreds of shimmering silver fish rush directly toward and across the camera.

They completely fill the frame.

19–23 SEC — GOLDEN DESERT

The silver fish seamlessly transform into thousands of glowing golden particles carried by desert wind.

Camera pushes through the particles.

Reveal my model walking along the crest of an enormous golden sand dune during sunset.

Her outfit has seamlessly transformed into an elegant flowing ivory desert outfit.

HER FACE AND IDENTITY MUST REMAIN EXACTLY THE SAME.

Wind dramatically moves the fabric.

CAMERA: Start extremely wide → rapidly fly toward her → smooth orbit around her body.

Reveal one final mysterious door standing alone at the top of the dune.

She opens it.

Brilliant golden-white sunlight bursts through.

23–28 SEC — ABOVE THE CLOUDS

She steps through the door.

Camera follows immediately.

Reveal an extraordinary mirrored.
#AICinema #Seedance25
```

</details>
